Endpoint paths are shown relative to the API base URL (`https://openrouter.ai/api/v1`).

<Update label="October 29, 2026" tags={["API Keys","BYOK","Breaking","Private Endpoints","Private Providers","Schemas"]} rss="1 breaking change, 5 new endpoints, 5 modified endpoints, 8 new schemas, 13 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `UpdatePrivateEndpointRequest`: required `upstream_model_id` property removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Used by [`PATCH /private-endpoints/{id}`](/api/api-reference/private-endpoints/update-a-private-endpoint)
  - **Migration: no action.** `PATCH /private-endpoints/{id}` is now a merge patch over every field an owner controls (`pricing`, `declared_parameters`, `settings`, and the draft-only upstream fields), so no single field is required and only the keys you send change. A request that still sends `upstream_model_id` is accepted on a draft endpoint; on an active endpoint it returns 409 `already_active`, as before. `PUT /private-endpoints/{id}/pricing` and `PUT /private-endpoints/{id}/parameters` keep working as deprecated aliases of this route.

## New endpoints

- [`GET /private-providers`](/api/api-reference/private-providers/list-private-providers): List private providers
- [`POST /private-providers`](/api/api-reference/private-providers/create-a-private-provider): Create a private provider
- [`GET /private-providers/{slug}`](/api/api-reference/private-providers/get-a-private-provider): Get a private provider
- [`PATCH /private-providers/{slug}`](/api/api-reference/private-providers/update-a-private-provider): Update a private provider
- [`DELETE /private-providers/{slug}`](/api/api-reference/private-providers/delete-a-private-provider): Delete a private provider

<Update label="October 28, 2026" tags={["API Keys","BYOK","Breaking","MCP","Models","Observability","Private Endpoints","Vault"]} rss="7 breaking changes, 5 modified endpoints, 8 new response codes, 2 new schemas, 13 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `ObservabilityS3Destination.config.roleArn`: pattern narrowed from `^arn:aws(?:-[a-z]+)*:iam::\d{12}:role\/[\w+=,.@/-]{1,512}$` to `^arn:aws:iam::\d{12}:role\/[\w+=,.@/-]{1,512}$`<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - **Migration: `roleArn` accepts only roles in the commercial AWS partition (`arn:aws:iam::`).** OpenRouter assumes your role from the commercial partition, which cannot assume roles in AWS GovCloud (`arn:aws-us-gov`) or China (`arn:aws-cn`), so destinations with those ARNs never delivered traces. `POST` and `PATCH` requests with such an ARN now fail validation; recreate the destination with a role in the commercial partition.
  - **Migration: the role's trust policy must require the destination's external ID.** In the same release, IAM role destinations started verifying that the trust policy carries an `sts:ExternalId` condition. Test Connection, Send Trace, and live delivery return 403 for a role that accepts any external ID; add the condition to the trust policy and the role is accepted within 5 minutes.

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/list-observability-destinations)
    - [`GET /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/get-an-observability-destination)
    - [`PATCH /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/update-an-observability-destination)
    - [`POST /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/create-an-observability-destination)
  </Expandable>
- `ChatDeveloperMessage.configuration_update`: additionalProperties `false` object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Used by [`POST /chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/chat/create-a-chat-completion), [`POST /presets/{slug}/chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/presets/create-a-preset-from-a-chat-completions-request-body)
  - **Migration: no action.** `configuration_update` on `ChatDeveloperMessage` is now a reference to the named `ConfigurationUpdate` schema instead of an inline object. The accepted value is unchanged: omit it, send `null`, or send an object with `reasoning`. Only generated SDK type names move; raw HTTP requests are unaffected.

## Modified endpoints

- [`POST /private-endpoints`](/api/api-reference/private-endpoints/create-a-private-endpoint): now returns 400 `parameters_invalid` when `declared_parameters` includes `tool_choice` without `tools`, matching `PUT /private-endpoints/{id}/parameters`; the create response does not echo `declared_parameters` or `tested_parameters`, read them back from `GET /private-endpoints/{id}`
- [`POST /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/create-a-new-api-key): request schema updated
- [`PATCH /keys/{hash}`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/update-an-api-key): request schema updated
- [`GET /vault/interns/{internId}/connections`](/api/api-reference/vault/list-the-connections-an-intern-can-use):
  - description updated
  - response schema updated
  - response now always includes `routing`
  - response now always includes `routing_text`
- [`GET /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/list-byok-provider-credentials): parameter `provider` updated

<Update label="October 27, 2026" tags={["Breaking","Endpoints","Generations","Models","Presets","Private Endpoints","Schemas"]} rss="2 breaking changes, 1 new endpoint, 1 modified endpoint, 10 new response codes, 4 new schemas, 10 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `ObservabilityS3Destination.config`: required `accessKeyId` property removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **Migration: no action for existing destinations.** S3 destinations now support IAM role authentication (`authMode: "iam_role"` with `provider: "aws"`, `roleArn`, and `region`), where OpenRouter assumes a role in your AWS account instead of storing access keys. `accessKeyId` and `secretAccessKey` are now optional in the schema because IAM role destinations have none. `authMode` defaults to `access_keys`, and in that mode both keys are still required on create and update, so existing requests and destinations behave exactly as before.
  - **Migration for clients that read destinations:** if your code or a generated SDK type assumes `config.accessKeyId` and `config.secretAccessKey` are always present in `GET /observability/destinations` responses, handle them as optional; they are absent on IAM role destinations. Check `config.authMode` to tell the two apart.

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/list-observability-destinations)
    - [`GET /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/get-an-observability-destination)
    - [`PATCH /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/update-an-observability-destination)
    - [`POST /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/create-an-observability-destination)
  </Expandable>
- `ObservabilityS3Destination.config`: required `secretAccessKey` property removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Same change as above: required only when `authMode` is `access_keys` (the default).

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/list-observability-destinations)
    - [`GET /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/get-an-observability-destination)

<Update label="October 21, 2026" tags={["Anthropic Messages","BYOK","Breaking","End Users","External Users","Guardrails","Interns","Organization","Private Endpoints","Workspaces"]} rss="14 breaking changes, 6 new endpoints, 10 modified endpoints, 19 new response codes, 7 new schemas, 12 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `CreateEndUserRequest`: schema object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Renamed to `CreateExternalUserRequest`; the shape is unchanged, so HTTP callers need no change. The Python SDK (`openrouter` 1.3.70 and later) and TypeScript SDK (`@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.0 and later) replace the `CreateEndUserRequest` model with `CreateExternalUserRequest`.
- `EndUser`: schema object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Renamed to `ExternalUser`; the shape is unchanged, so HTTP callers need no change. The Python SDK (`openrouter` 1.3.70 and later) and TypeScript SDK (`@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.0 and later) replace the `EndUser` model with `ExternalUser`.
- `EndUserResponse`: schema object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Renamed to `ExternalUserResponse`; the shape is unchanged, so HTTP callers need no change. The Python SDK (`openrouter` 1.3.70 and later) and TypeScript SDK (`@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.0 and later) replace the `EndUserResponse` model with `ExternalUserResponse`.
- `ListEndUsersResponse`: schema object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Renamed to `ListExternalUsersResponse`; the shape is unchanged, so HTTP callers need no change. The Python SDK (`openrouter` 1.3.70 and later) and TypeScript SDK (`@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.0 and later) replace the `ListEndUsersResponse` model with `ListExternalUsersResponse`.
- `UpdateEndUserRequest`: schema object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Renamed to `UpdateExternalUserRequest`; the shape is unchanged, so HTTP callers need no change. The Python SDK (`openrouter` 1.3.70 and later) and TypeScript SDK (`@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.0 and later) replace the `UpdateEndUserRequest` model with `UpdateExternalUserRequest`.
- `/end-users`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Requests to `/end-users` continue to work and are forwarded to [`/external-users`](/api/api-reference/external-users/list-registered-external-users), so HTTP callers need no change. In the Python SDK (`openrouter` 1.3.70 and later), switch `client.end_users` to `client.external_users`; the old namespace was removed. In the TypeScript SDK (`@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.0 and later), use `externalUsers` for new code; `endUsers` remains available as a compatibility alias. Method names (`list`, `create`, `get`, `update`, `delete`) are unchanged.
- `/end-users/{user}`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Requests to `/end-users/{user}` continue to work and are forwarded to [`/external-users/{user}`](/api/api-reference/external-users/get-a-registered-external-user), so HTTP callers need no change. Python SDK users switch to `external_users`; TypeScript SDK users can migrate to `externalUsers` while `endUsers` remains available as a compatibility alias, as described on the `/end-users` entry above.
- `GET /interns/{internId}/icon`: content `image/jpeg` object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - The response is now declared as `image/*`. The bytes served and their `Content-Type` headers are unchanged.
- `GET /interns/{internId}/icon`: content `image/png` object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - The response is now declared as `image/*`. The bytes served and their `Content-Type` headers are unchanged.
- $.tags: End Users object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Documentation tag renamed to External Users. No API change.
- [`POST /messages`](/api/api-reference/anthropic-messages/create-a-message): x-speakeasy-sse-sentinel `[DONE]` object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Streams now end at the `message_stop` event and no longer send a trailing `data: [DONE]` frame, matching the Anthropic Messages protocol. Clients that detected the end of a `/messages` stream by waiting for `[DONE]` must terminate on `message_stop` on success, and on the `error` event or stream closure on failure. Anthropic's official SDKs already expect this.
- `BulkAssignMembersRequest`: `member_user_ids` now accepts at most 500 items<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Requests with more than 500 member user IDs now return `400`; previously they were accepted. Split larger lists into batches of 500 or fewer, matching the existing limit on the key assignment endpoints.
  - Used by [`POST /guardrails/{id}/assignments/members`](/api/api-reference/guardrails/bulk-assign-members-to-a-guardrail)
- `BulkUnassignMembersRequest`: `member_user_ids` now accepts at most 500 items<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Requests with more than 500 member user IDs now return `400`; previously they were accepted. Split larger lists into batches of 500 or fewer, matching the existing limit on the key assignment endpoints.
  - Used by [`POST /guardrails/{id}/assignments/members/remove`](/api/api-reference/guardrails/bulk-unassign-members-from-a-guardrail)
- `InternDaemonAccess`: required `viewer` property added<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Used by `GET /interns/{internId}/daemon`, `GET /interns/{internId}/daemon-access`

<Update label="October 6, 2026" tags={["API Keys","BYOK","Breaking","Schemas"]} rss="5 breaking changes, 8 modified endpoints, 1 new response code, 2 new schemas, 9 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `BYOKKey`: required `declared_region` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **No action required for existing consumers.** Responses from BYOK endpoints now include `declared_region` (`"global"`, `"europe"`, `"us"`, or `null`) indicating the key's declared data region.

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/list-byok-provider-credentials)
    - [`GET /byok/{id}`](/api/api-reference/byok/get-a-byok-provider-credential)
    - [`PATCH /byok/{id}`](/api/api-reference/byok/update-a-byok-provider-credential)
    - [`POST /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/create-a-byok-provider-credential)
  </Expandable>
- [`GET /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/list-api-keys): required `last_used_at` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - No action required for existing consumers. API key responses now include `last_used_at`, an ISO 8601 UTC timestamp of the key's most recent recorded usage, or `null` if none has been recorded.
- [`POST /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/create-a-new-api-key): required `last_used_at` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- [`GET /keys/{hash}`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/get-a-single-api-key): required `last_used_at` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- [`PATCH /keys/{hash}`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/update-an-api-key): required `last_used_at` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>

<Update label="September 19, 2026" tags={["API Keys","BYOK","Breaking","Interns","Observability","Schemas"]} rss="5 breaking changes, 6 modified endpoints, 3 new response codes, 1 new schema, 6 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- [`GET /key`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/get-current-api-key): required `organization_id` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- [`GET /key`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/get-current-api-key): required `workspace_id` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- `CreateInternRequest`: required `workspace_id` property removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Used by `POST /interns`
- `InternChatAssistantMessage`: required `content` property removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Used by `POST /interns/{internId}/chat/completions`
- `DELETE /interns/{internId}`: requestBody `requestBody` property added<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Regenerated SDK clients: pass `{}` when no acknowledgement is needed, or `{ acknowledge_workspace_loss: true }` when deleting the intern also requires acknowledging workspace loss. Raw HTTP clients may continue to omit the body.

## Modified endpoints

- [`GET /key`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/get-current-api-key): response schema updated
- `POST /interns`: description updated; response schema updated
- `POST /interns/{internId}/chat/completions`: description updated
- `DELETE /interns/{internId}`: description updated
- `GET /interns`: new parameter `starting_after`
- [`PATCH /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/update-an-observability-destination): description updated; response schema updated

## New response codes

- `DELETE /interns/{internId}`: now returns `400`
- `DELETE /interns/{internId}`: now returns `413`
- [`PATCH /byok/{id}`](/api/api-reference/byok/update-a-byok-provider-credential): now returns `403`

## New schemas

- `DeleteInternRequest`
  - Used by `DELETE /interns/{internId}`

## Modified schemas

- `InternChatCompletionRequest`: description updated
  - Used by `POST /interns/{internId}/chat/completions`
- `CreateInternRequest`: description updated
  - Used by `POST /interns`
- `TokenExchangeRequest`: pattern property_added
  - Used by [`POST /oauth/token`](/api/api-reference/oauth/exchange-a-workload-identity-token)
- `InternChatCompletionChunk`: description updated
  - Used by `POST /interns/{internId}/chat/completions`
- `UpdateInternRequest`: description updated; minLength property_added; pattern property_added
  - Used by `PATCH /interns/{internId}`
- `InternListResponse`: new property `next_cursor`
  - Used by `GET /interns`

</Update>

<Update label="September 10, 2026" tags={["Breaking","Workspaces"]} rss="2 breaking changes, 4 new endpoints.">

## Breaking changes

- `/workspaces/{id}/budgets`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - SDK and Terraform callers: rename the path argument from `id` to `workspace_ref` (`workspaceRef` in camelCase clients). The URL value itself is unchanged.
- `/workspaces/{id}/budgets/{interval}`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - SDK and Terraform callers: rename the path argument from `id` to `workspace_ref` (`workspaceRef` in camelCase clients). The URL value itself is unchanged.

## New endpoints

- [`GET /workspaces/{workspace_ref}/budgets`](/api/api-reference/workspaces/list-workspace-budgets): List workspace budgets
- [`GET /workspaces/{workspace_ref}/budgets/{interval}`](/api/api-reference/workspaces/get-a-workspace-budget): Get a workspace budget
- [`PUT /workspaces/{workspace_ref}/budgets/{interval}`](/api/api-reference/workspaces/create-or-update-a-workspace-budget): Create or update a workspace budget
- [`DELETE /workspaces/{workspace_ref}/budgets/{interval}`](/api/api-reference/workspaces/delete-a-workspace-budget): Delete a workspace budget

</Update>

<Update label="August 24, 2026" tags={["API Keys","Breaking","Containers","Schemas"]} rss="9 breaking changes, 6 new endpoints, 7 modified endpoints, 2 new schemas, 11 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `ContainerReferenceEnvironment`: maxLength `20` modified<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - No action required for existing consumers. The maximum container reference length increased from 20 to 40 characters.

  <Expandable title="6 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`POST /chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/chat/create-a-chat-completion)
    - [`POST /messages`](/api/api-reference/anthropic-messages/create-a-message)
    - [`POST /presets/{slug}/chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/presets/create-a-preset-from-a-chat-completions-request-body)
    - [`POST /presets/{slug}/messages`](/api/api-reference/presets/create-a-preset-from-a-messages-request-body)
    - [`POST /presets/{slug}/responses`](/api/api-reference/presets/create-a-preset-from-a-responses-request-body)
    - [`POST /responses`](/api/api-reference/responses/create-a-response)
  </Expandable>
- `/containers/{session_id}/files`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Use the corresponding `/containers/{container_id}/...` endpoint and pass the canonical `container_id` returned by a bash or shell tool result instead of the original session ID.
- `/containers/{session_id}/files/{file_id}`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Use the corresponding `/containers/{container_id}/...` endpoint and pass the canonical `container_id` returned by a bash or shell tool result instead of the original session ID.
- `/containers/{session_id}/files/{file_id}/content`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Use the corresponding `/containers/{container_id}/...` endpoint and pass the canonical `container_id` returned by a bash or shell tool result instead of the original session ID.
- `ObservabilityDatadogDestination`: default `https://api.us5.datadoghq.com` modified<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - If your Datadog site is US5 and you relied on the omitted URL default, explicitly set `url` to `https://api.us5.datadoghq.com`. The new default is the US1 endpoint, `https://api.datadoghq.com`.

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/list-observability-destinations)

    - [`PATCH /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/update-an-observability-destination)
    - [`POST /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/create-an-observability-destination)
  </Expandable>
- [`GET /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/list-api-keys): required `external_user` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- [`POST /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/create-a-new-api-key): required `external_user` property added<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Connect client-secret callers must include a non-empty `external_user` when creating a key. Management-key callers should continue to omit this Connect-only field.
- [`GET /keys/{hash}`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/get-a-single-api-key): required `external_user` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>

<Update label="August 7, 2026" tags={["BYOK","Breaking","Observability","Schemas"]} rss="56 breaking changes, 6 modified endpoints, 1 new response code, 1 new schema, 40 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `BYOKKey`: type `string` modified<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Allow `workspace_id` to be `null` in BYOK responses. A null value identifies a global credential that is usable across every workspace; do not substitute the default workspace.

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/list-byok-provider-credentials)
    - [`GET /byok/{id}`](/api/api-reference/byok/get-a-byok-provider-credential)
    - [`PATCH /byok/{id}`](/api/api-reference/byok/update-a-byok-provider-credential)
    - [`POST /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/create-a-byok-provider-credential)
  </Expandable>
- `Guardrail`: type `string` modified<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Allow `workspace_id` to be `null` in guardrail responses. A null value identifies an unscoped legacy guardrail; it does not mean the default workspace or every workspace.

<Update label="August 3, 2026" tags={["Analytics","Breaking","Endpoints","beta.Analytics"]} rss="1 breaking change, 3 modified endpoints, 4 new schemas, 4 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `GenerationResponse`: required `workspace_id` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - No action required for existing consumers. Generation responses now include `workspace_id`, the ID of the workspace the generation belongs to.
  - Used by [`GET /generation`](/api/api-reference/generations/get-request-&-usage-metadata-for-a-generation)

- [`GET /activity`](/api/api-reference/analytics/get-user-activity-grouped-by-endpoint):

  - new parameter `group_by`

## Breaking changes

- `FileMetadata`: schema object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- `FileDeleteResponse`: discriminator `discriminator` object added<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - HTTP clients: no change. Requests without a vendor SDK still get the OpenRouter shape (`_shape: "openrouter"`) with the same fields as before. Typed SDK clients: this response type is now a union discriminated by `_shape`. <Update label="July 28, 2026" tags={["Breaking","Files","Responses","Schemas"]} rss="2 breaking changes, 5 modified endpoints, 22 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `FusionCallAnalysisInProgressEvent`: required `analyst_model` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **No action required for existing consumers.** `analyst_model` replaces `judge_model` in this streaming event; `judge_model` is retained as a deprecated alias that always carries the same value.
  - Used by [`POST /responses`](/api/api-reference/responses/create-a-response)
- $.tags: responses object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **No action required.** The `responses` OpenAPI tag was renamed to `Responses` for consistent Title Case across the API Reference. This is a documentation-grouping change only: `POST /api/v1/responses` and its schemas are unchanged, SDK namespaces are unchanged (`client.responses.send()` in TypeScript and Python, `Sdk.Responses.Send()` in Go), and endpoint documentation URLs are unchanged.

<Update label="July 25, 2026" tags={["BYOK","Breaking","Responses","Schemas"]} rss="2 breaking changes, 2 modified endpoints, 5 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- [`POST /responses`](/api/api-reference/responses/create-a-response): tags `beta.responses` property removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **Migration: no action for HTTP clients.** The Responses API is now GA. `beta.responses` was an OpenAPI grouping tag and SDK namespace, never a URL. `POST /api/v1/responses` and its request and response schemas are unchanged, so raw HTTP callers and OpenAI-compatible clients (including the OpenAI Agents SDK) need no changes. The endpoint now appears under **Responses** instead of **Beta responses** in the API Reference.
  - **Migration for SDK users: optional until sunset.** The canonical namespace is now `responses`, with `client.responses.send()` (TypeScript, Python) and `client.Responses.Send()` (Go). The `beta.responses` namespace, and TypeScript's `betaResponsesSend` standalone function, keep working as deprecated aliases; their removal will be announced here with a sunset date before it ships.
- $.tags: beta.responses object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Same change as above at the specification level: the `beta.responses` tag definition is gone from `$.tags`.

## Modified endpoints

- [`POST /responses`](/api/api-reference/responses/create-a-response): tags property_added
- [`GET /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/list-byok-provider-credentials): parameter `provider` updated

## Modified schemas

- `MessagesStartEvent`: property `provider` updated
  - Used by [`POST /messages`](/api/api-reference/anthropic-messages/create-a-message)
- `ProviderName`: enum property_added

The `name` and `type` properties moved into that variant rather than being removed.
